No exception, no test-visible error, and every functional assertion\nstill passes, because \"rejected\" is the *expected* result for a non-existent account. Only the\ntiming differs.\n\n**How to apply:**\n- Build the dummy value from a real KDF run (e.g. `hasher.dummyEncoded()` deriving over a\n  password that matches nothing) instead of writing the literal.\n- Assert the digest *parses* (`storedIterations(dummy) == expected`) and that verifying against it\n  takes measurable time (`>= a few ms`), not merely that it returns false.\n- When timing both branches, isolate them: per-account lockout (e.g. 5 strikes) will short-circuit\n  later iterations and fabricate a ratio. The helper that derived the key wiped its local salt\n   in a `finally` block, which zeroed the salt the already-returned envelope still pointed at, so\n   every subsequent decrypt derived a different key and failed the GCM tag check. Fix: clone in the\n   canonical constructor *and* return a clone from the accessor; also override `equals`/`hashCode`\n   (the generated ones use `IdentityHashMap`-style reference equality for array components, so two\n   logically equal derivations are unequal) and `toString` if the array is key-adjacent.\n\n2. **`SecretKeySpec.destroy()` throws `DestroyFailedException` and destroys nothing**, and\n   `isDestroyed()` stays false. The interface `javax.crypto.SecretKey extends Destroyable` makes it\n   look usable; it is not. Zeroing the `byte[]` copies is the only erasure available, so do not\n   write code that calls `destroy()` and assumes the key is gone.\n\nAlso confirmed: `new SecretKeySpec(bytes, \"AES\")` copies the input array, and `getEncoded()` returns\na fresh copy each call - so wiping a `getEncoded()` result never corrupts a key another caller\nstill holds (worth checking before \"fixing\" a suspected aliasing bug; it was the wrong hypothesis\nhere, the real one was (1)).\n\n**Why:** (1) cost a debugging cycle - the symptom (tag mismatch on a passphrase round trip) pointed\nat the cipher, not at a record constructor, and nothing in the JDK warns about shared record\ncomponents. (2) means a `finally { key.destroy(); }` is decorative.\n\n**How to apply:** Whenever a key, salt, IV or passphrase crosses an API boundary in Java, decide\nexplicitly who owns the array, clone on the way in if the object outlives the call, and wipe only\nbuffers you own. Add a check that the value survives after the producer has wiped its own copy -\nthat is what pins this class of bug.\n\nRelated: `jdk-lenient-key-and-base64-apis` (the same class of \"the JDK does not validate key\nmaterial for you\").\n"}]}},{"type":"user","uuid":"5e91f68c-683b-494d-bbe0-7e2f18af9f62","session_id":"a1b384c5-81d6-4817-8527-4740edcd861f","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"call_f3a9442cea904154afb4028a","is_error":false,"content":"---\nname: SecretKeySpec and the MIME Base64 decoder are both lenient\ndescription: JDK gotchas - SecretKeySpec accepts a wrong-length AES key and Base64.getMimeDecoder() silently discards junk, so validate key length and alphabet yourself\ntype: reference\n---\n\nTwo JDK APIs that refuse to reject bad key material, verified on OpenJDK 17.0.20.1:\n\n1. `new SecretKeySpec(bytes, \"AES\")` only rejects an *empty* array. A 8- or 20-byte\n   \"AES key\" constructs fine; the failure is deferred to `Cipher.init` as an\n   `InvalidKeyException`. So if you want \"rejected at the boundary with a clear\n   message\", check `length == 16 || 24 || 32` explicitly.\n\n2. `Base64.getMimeDecoder().decode(...)` ignores every character outside the alphabet\n   rather than throwing. A junk string like `\"not-a-key!\"` decodes to the bytes of\n   `\"notakey\"` with no error, which silently becomes a weak/truncated key. Use the\n   strict `Base64.getDecoder()` (falling back to `getUrlDecoder()` if you must accept\n   both alphabets), and tolerate whitespace by stripping it yourself.\n\n**Why:** Both were caught only by a self test written to expect rejection - the\nconstruct-then-fail-later order makes the bug invisible at the call site, and the MIME\ndecoder's junk tolerance is undocumented behaviour people routinely assume is validation.\n\n**How to apply:** When accepting key material from a CLI argument, config file or env\nvar, decode with a strict decoder and assert the decoded byte length before wrapping it\nin a `SecretKeySpec`. java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;\nimport java.util.regex.Pattern;\nimport javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory;\nimport javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec;\n\n/**\n * Password authentication service with an audit log.\n *\n * <p>Public API: {@link #register(String, char[])}, {@link #authenticate(String, char[])} and\n * {@link #authenticate(String, char[], String)}. An {@link Result} carries the caller-facing\n * outcome plus an internal {@link Reason} that is meant for the audit trail only.\n *\n * <p>Design points that are load bearing and easy to get wrong:\n * <ul>\n *   <li>Passwords are stored only as PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 digests with a per-account random salt\n *       (self-describing encoding, see {@link PasswordHash#encode()}). The plaintext password is\n *       never stored, logged or returned.</li>\n *   <li>Every attempt derives exactly one key, including attempts that are refused before the\n *       credential check (unknown user, malformed username, malformed password, corrupt record,\n *       disabled account, locked account). Without that, response time alone tells an attacker\n *       which usernames exist. The equal-work value is generated at construction time by a real\n *       key derivation, never pasted in as a literal.</li>\n *   <li>Key comparison is constant time ({@link MessageDigest#isEqual(byte[], byte[])}).</li>\n *   <li>Candidates that the JCE would silently collapse onto another password are rejected before\n *       hashing: {@link #isHashablePassword(char[])} explains why.</li>\n *   <li>Every attempt is written to an {@link EventLogger}. Log fields are sanitized so that a\n *       username containing CR/LF cannot forge a log record.</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * <p>See README.md for compile/run instructions and for the documented limitations.\n */\npublic final class UserAuthentication {\n\n    /** Caller-facing outcome. Deliberately does not distinguish \"no such user\" from \"wrong\n     * password\", so a login screen can render one message for both. */\n    public enum Outcome {\n        SUCCESS,\n        INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n        ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n        ACCOUNT_DISABLED,\n        INVALID_INPUT\n    }\n\n    /** Internal cause. Audit-trail data: rendering this to an end user would leak whether an\n     * account exists. */\n    public enum Reason {\n        OK,\n        UNKNOWN_USER,\n        BAD_PASSWORD,\n        ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n        ACCOUNT_DISABLED,\n        MALFORMED_USERNAME,\n        MALFORMED_PASSWORD,\n        CORRUPT_RECORD\n    }\n\n    /** Event kinds written to the application log. */\n    public enum EventType {\n        LOGIN_SUCCESS,\n        LOGIN_FAILURE,\n        LOGIN_REFUSED,\n        ACCOUNT_REGISTERED,\n        ACCOUNT_LOCKED_OUT,\n        ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED\n    }\n\n    public static final int DEFAULT_ITERATIONS = 120_000;\n    private static final int SALT_BYTES = 16;\n    private static final int KEY_BYTES = 32;\n    private static final String KDF = \"PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256\";\n    private static final String HASH_PREFIX = \"pbkdf2-sha256\";\n    private static final int USERNAME_MIN = 3;\n    private static final int USERNAME_MAX = 64;\n    private static final int PASSWORD_MAX = 1024;\n    private static final Pattern USERNAME_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(\"[a-z0-9._-]+\");\n    /** Password used for the equal-work derivation. It matches no stored account. */\n    private static final char[] EQUAL_WORK_SECRET =\n        \"equal-work-placeholder-never-a-real-secret\".toCharArray();\n\n    private final AccountStore accounts;\n    private final EventLogger log;\n    private final Config config;\n    private final SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();\n    /** Real digest of {@link #EQUAL_WORK_SECRET} at {@link Config#iterations}: the work unit that\n     * every refused attempt has to pay. */\n    private final PasswordHash equalWorkHash;\n\n    public UserAuthentication(AccountStore accounts, EventLogger log, Config config) {\n        if (accounts == null || log == null || config == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"accounts, log and config are required\");\n        }\n        this.accounts = accounts;\n        this.log = log;\n        this.config = config;\n        this.equalWorkHash = PasswordHash.generate(EQUAL_WORK_SECRET, config.iterations, new SecureRandom());\n    }\n\n    /** Service backed by an in-memory store, handy for tests, demos and single-process apps. */\n    public static UserAuthentication withInMemoryStore(EventLogger log, Config config) {\n        return new UserAuthentication(new InMemoryAccountStore(), log, config);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Configuration\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /** Tunables. Lower the iteration count only in tests; 120k is a reasonable 2026 baseline. */\n    public static final class Config {\n        public final int iterations;\n        public final int maxFailures;\n        public final Duration lockDuration;\n        public final Duration failureWindow;\n        public final Clock clock;\n\n        public Config(int iterations, int maxFailures, Duration lockDuration,\n                      Duration failureWindow, Clock clock) {\n            if (iterations < 1_000) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"iterations must be >= 1000, got \" + iterations);\n            }\n            if (maxFailures < 1) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"maxFailures must be >= 1, got \" + maxFailures);\n            }\n            if (lockDuration == null || lockDuration.isNegative() || lockDuration.isZero()) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"lockDuration must be positive\");\n            }\n            if (failureWindow == null || failureWindow.isNegative() || failureWindow.isZero()) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"failureWindow must be positive\");\n            }\n            this.iterations = iterations;\n            this.maxFailures = maxFailures;\n            this.lockDuration = lockDuration;\n            this.failureWindow = failureWindow;\n            this.clock = clock == null ? Clock.systemUTC() : clock;\n        }\n\n        public static Config defaults() {\n            return new Config(DEFAULT_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(15),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(30), Clock.systemUTC());\n        }\n\n        /** Same lockout policy, fewer KDF rounds: keeps test suites and demos fast. */\n        public static Config fast(int iterations) {\n            return new Config(iterations, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(30),\n                Clock.systemUTC());\n        }\n\n        /** Non-locking policy for suites that want many failed attempts on one account. */\n        public Config withoutLockout(int strikes) {\n            return new Config(iterations, strikes, lockDuration, failureWindow, clock);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Result / log record value types\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /** Immutable outcome of one {@code authenticate} or {@code register} call. */\n    public static final class Result {\n        private final Outcome outcome;\n        private final Reason reason;\n        private final String username;\n        private final Instant timestamp;\n        private final long durationMillis;\n        private final int failedAttempts;\n\n        Result(Outcome outcome, Reason reason, String username, Instant timestamp,\n               long durationMillis, int failedAttempts) {\n            this.outcome = outcome;\n            this.reason = reason;\n            this.username = username;\n            this.timestamp = timestamp;\n            this.durationMillis = durationMillis;\n            this.failedAttempts = failedAttempts;\n        }\n\n        public Outcome outcome() {\n            return outcome;\n        }\n\n        /** Audit-only cause; Field values are already sanitized: ASCII, no CR/LF, no '|'. */\n    public static final class Event {\n        public final Instant timestamp;\n        public final EventType type;\n        public final String username;\n        public final Outcome outcome;\n        public final Reason reason;\n        public final String remoteAddress;\n        public final long durationMillis;\n        public final int failedAttempts;\n        public final String detail;\n\n        Event(EventType type, String username, Outcome outcome, Reason reason,\n              String remoteAddress, long durationMillis, int failedAttempts, String detail) {\n            this.timestamp = Instant.now();\n            this.type = type;\n            this.username = sanitizeField(username);\n            this.outcome = outcome;\n            this.reason = reason;\n            this.remoteAddress = sanitizeField(remoteAddress);\n            this.durationMillis = durationMillis;\n            this.failedAttempts = failedAttempts;\n            this.detail = sanitizeField(detail);\n        }\n\n        /** Single-line pipe separated record; {@link #parseLine(String)} reads it back. */\n        public String format() {\n            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(160);\n            sb.append(DateTimeFormatter.ISO_INSTANT.format(timestamp.truncatedTo(java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit.MILLIS)));\n            sb.append(\"|event=\").append(type);\n            sb.append(\"|user=\").append(username);\n            sb.append(\"|outcome=\").append(outcome == null ? Returns null if unparseable. */\n        public static Event parseLine(String line) {\n            if (line == null) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            String[] parts = line.split(\"\\\\|\");\n            if (parts.length < 9) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            try {\n                EventType type = EventType.valueOf(valueOf(parts[1], \"event=\"));\n                Outcome outcome = \"-\".equals(valueOf(parts[3], \"outcome=\")) ? null\n                    : Outcome.valueOf(valueOf(parts[3], \"outcome=\"));\n                Reason reason = \"-\".equals(valueOf(parts[4], \"reason=\")) ? null\n                    : Reason.valueOf(valueOf(parts[4], \"reason=\"));\n                return new Event(type, valueOf(parts[2], \"user=\"), outcome, reason,\n                    valueOf(parts[5], \"addr=\"), Long.parseLong(valueOf(parts[6], \"durationMs=\")),\n                    Integer.parseInt(valueOf(parts[7], \"failures=\")), valueOf(parts[8], \"detail=\"));\n            } catch (RuntimeException malformed) {\n                return null;\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static String valueOf(String field, String prefix) {\n            return field.startsWith(prefix) ? field.substring(prefix.length()) : field;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Sink for authentication activity. Implementations must be thread safe. */\n    public interface EventLogger {\n        void log(Event event);\n\n        /** Discards everything; useful when a caller has its own audit pipeline. */\n        static EventLogger nullLogger() {\n            return event -> {\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Console logger. */\n    public static final class ConsoleEventLogger implements EventLogger {\n        private final PrintStream out;\n\n        public ConsoleEventLogger() {\n            this(System.out);\n        }\n\n        public ConsoleEventLogger(PrintStream out) {\n            this.out = out;\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void log(Event event) {\n            out.println(event.format());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Appends one flushed line per event to a file, so a crash cannot lose the last attempt.\n     * Writing and flushing happen under the instance lock, which keeps concurrent logins from\n     * interleaving partial lines.\n     */\n    public static final class FileEventLogger implements EventLogger, AutoCloseable {\n        private final Object lock = new Object();\n        private final Path file;\n        private final PrintStream echo;\n        private BufferedWriter writer;\n\n        public FileEventLogger(Path file) {\n            this(file, null);\n        }\n\n        public FileEventLogger(Path file, PrintStream echo) {\n            if (file == null) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"file is required\");\n            }\n            this.file = file;\n            this.echo = echo;\n            try {\n                if (file.getParent() != null) {\n                    Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());\n                }\n                this.writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII,\n                    StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.WRITE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND);\n            } catch (IOException cannotOpen) {\n                throw new UncheckedIOException(\"cannot open audit log \" + file, cannotOpen);\n            }\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public void log(Event event) {\n            String line = event.format();\n            synchronized (lock) {\n                try {\n                    writer.write(line);\n                    writer.newLine();\n                    writer.flush();\n                } catch (IOException writeFailed) {\n                    throw new UncheckedIOException(\"cannot write audit log \" + file, writeFailed);\n                }\n   /**\n     * Verifies one credential pair and records the attempt.\n     *\n     * @param remoteAddress optional client address, recorded verbatim (sanitized) in the log\n     */\n    public Result remoteAddress, startedAt, nanos, 0, \"account disabled\", null);\n        }\n\n        Account.LockState lockState = account.lockStateAt(startedAt);\n        if (lockState == Account.LockState.ACTIVE)    \"locked for another \" + account.lockRemainingSeconds(startedAt) + \"s\", null);\n        }\n        if (lockState == Account.LockState.EXPIRED) {\n            log.log(new Event(EventType.ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED, username, null, Reason.OK, remoteAddress,\n                elapsedMillis(nanos), 0, \"lock expired\", null));\n        }\n\n        if (!stored.matches(presented))         cleared > 0 ? \"prior failures cleared\" : \"ok\", null));\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    /** Enables or disables an account; disabled accounts are refused before any credential use. */\n    public boolean setEnabled(String username, boolean enabled) {\n        String name = normalizeUsername(username);\n        Account account = name == null ? null : accounts.find(name);\n Distinct passwords\n     * therefore collide on the same digest, which would let one holder of \"pw\" log in as the\n     * holder of \"pw\\0\". Those forms are rejected rather than silently trimmed, because trimming\n     * changes the secret.          .chars().count() == 3 && UserAuthentication.isHashablePassword(\n            \"a\" + (char) 0xD83D + (char) 0xDE00).toCharArray());\n    }\n\n    private static void passwordForms() {\n checkTrue(\"trailing NUL rejected\",\n            !UserAuthentication.isHashablePassword(new char[] {'a', 'b', 'c', 0}));\n        checkTrue(\"lone high surrogate rejected\",\n       UserAuthentication.PasswordHash.generate(\"same-password\".toCharArray(), FAST_ITERATIONS,\n                new java.security.SecureRandom());\n        UserAuthentication.PasswordHash h2 =\n            UserAuthentication.PasswordHash.generate(\"same-password\".toCharArray(), FAST_ITERATIONS,\n                new java.security.SecureRandom());\n        check(\"two registrations of the same h1.matches(h1.deriveKey(\"same-password\".toCharArray())));\n        checkTrue(\"but not the other one's\", !h1.matches(h2.deriveKey(\"same-password\".toCharArray())));\n\n        UserAuthentication.PasswordHash parsed = UserAuthentication.PasswordHash.parse(h1.encode());\n        check(\"encoding round-trips the iteration count\", h1.iterations(), parsed.iterations());\n        checkTrue(\"encoding round-trips the salt\", Arrays.equals(h1.salt(), parsed.salt()));\n        checkTrue(\"parsed record verifies the real password\",\n            parsed.matches(parsed.deriveKey(\"same-password\".toCharArray())));\n        checkTrue(\"parsed record rejects a wrong password\",\n            !parsed.matches(parsed.deriveKey(\"wrong-password\".toCharArray())));\n        check(\"toString does not leak material\", \"PasswordHash[redacted]\", parsed.toString());\n\n        byte[] salt = h1.matches(h1.deriveKey(\"same-password\".toCharArray())));\n\n        for (String bad : new String[] {\n            null,\n            \"\",\n            \"bcrypt$10$abc$abc\",\n hash rejected: \" + bad, throwsIAE(() -> UserAuthentication.PasswordHash.parse(bad)));\n        }\n        checkTrue(\"short salt rejected\", throwsIAE(() -> UserAuthentication.PasswordHash\n .parse(\"pbkdf2-sha256$2000$AAAAAAAA$AAAA\")));\n\n        // The JCE's char[] converter is lossy; the guard is what keeps two different passwords from\n        // sharing one digest. Assert the collision the guard prevents, using the raw JCE.\n        String[] collide = {\"password-9\", \"password-9\" + (char) 0, \"password-9\" + (char) 0 + (char) 0};\n        checkTrue(\"raw JCE collapses trailing-NUL variants (why the guard exists)\",\n            Arrays.equals(rawDerive(collide[0].toCharArray(), h1.salt(), FAST_ITERATIONS),\n                rawDerive(collide[1].toCharArray(), h1.salt(), FAST_ITERATIONS)));\n        for (String candidate : collide) {\n            checkTrue(\"service refuses the collapsing form: [\" + candidate.length() + \" chars]\",\n                !UserAuthentication.isHashablePassword(candidate.toCharArray()));\n        }\n        String surrogates = \"pw\" + (char) 0xD800;\n        checkTrue(\"raw JCE collapses must still be usable.\n        UserAuthentication.PasswordHash astral = UserAuthentication.PasswordHash.generate(\n            (\"pw\" + (char) 0xD83D + (char) 0xDE00).toCharArray(), 0xDE00).toCharArray())));\n    }\n\n    private static byte[] rawDerive(char[] password, byte[] salt, int iterations) {\n        try {\n            javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec spec =\n                new javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec(password, salt, iterations, 256);\n            return javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory.getInstance(\"PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256\")\n                .generateSecret(spec).getEncoded();\n        } catch (java.security.GeneralSecurityException impossible) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(impossible);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void credentials() {\n        AccountStore store = freshStore();\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(store, Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(NO_LOCKOUT), log);\n        checkTrue(\"alice registered\", auth.register(\"alice\", PW));\n        checkFalse(\"duplicate registration refused\", auth.register(\"alice\", \"other-password\".toCharArray()));\n        check(\"account exists after registration\", true, auth.accountExists(\"ALICE \"));\n        check(\"unknown account does not exist\", false, auth.accountExists(\"nobody\"));\n\n        UserAuthentication.Result ok = auth.authenticate(\"alice\", PW);\n        check(\"correct password succeeds\", Outcome.SUCCESS, ok.outcome());\n        check(\"success reason is OK\", UserAuthentication.Reason.OK, ok.auditReason());\n        checkTrue(\"success reports zero failures\", ok.failedAttempts() == 0);\n        checkTrue(\"success timestamp is populated\", ok.timestamp() != null);\n        check(\"user message on success\", \"Login successful.\", ok.userMessage());\n\n        String[] wrongPasswords = {\"alice\", \"Alice1\", \"winter-Frost-41\", \"winter-Frost-42 \", \" \",\n            \"winter\", \"WINTER-FROST-42\", \"hunter2-nope\", \"00000000\", \"password-9\", \"correct horse\"};\n        for (String wrong : wrongPasswords) {\n            UserAuthentication.Result bad = auth.authenticate(\"alice\", wrong.toCharArray());\n            check(\"wrong password [\" + wrong.length() + \" chars] refused\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n                bad.outcome());\n            check(\"wrong password [\" + wrong.length() + \" chars] reason\",\n                UserAuthentication.Reason.BAD_PASSWORD, bad.auditReason());\n        }\n        check(\"strikes accumulated by the matrix\", wrongPasswords.length,\n            store.failuresOf(\"alice\"));\n\n        UserAuthentication.Result unknown = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", \"whatever-123\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"unknown user refused\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, unknown.outcome());\n        check(\"unknown user reason\", UserAuthentication.Reason.UNKNOWN_USER, unknown.auditReason());\n        check(\"unknown user is not registered as a side effect\", false, auth.accountExists(\"carol\"));\n\n        checkTrue(\"the two refusal messages are indistinguishable\",\n            unknown.userMessage().equals(auth.authenticate(\"alice\", \"nope-nope-nope\".toCharArray()).userMessage()));\n        check(\"generic message text\", \"Invalid username or password.\", unknown.userMessage());\n\n        checkTrue(\"password is case sensitive\", auth.authenticate(\"alice\", PW).isSuccess());\n        checkFalse(\"all-caps password refused\",\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", \"WINTER-FROST-42\".toCharArray()).isSuccess());\n        checkFalse(\"password with a trailing NUL refused outright\",\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (new String(PW) + (char) 0).toCharArray()).isSuccess());\n        check(\"trailing NUL candidate is refused as invalid input\", Outcome.INVALID_INPUT,\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (new String(PW) + (char) 0).toCharArray()).outcome());\n        check(\"unpaired-surrogate candidate is refused as invalid input\", Outcome.INVALID_INPUT,\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", new char[] {'w', (char) 0xD800}).outcome());\n        check(\"empty password is invalid input, not a credential check\", Outcome.INVALID_INPUT,\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", new char[0]).outcome());\n        check(\"oversized password is invalid input\", Outcome.INVALID_INPUT,\n            auth.authenticate(\"alice\", new char[4096]).outcome());\n\n        // A login must work for every registration path, including a normalized alias.\n        UserAuthentication auth2 = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), new RecordingLog());\n        checkTrue(\"register through a messy name\", auth2.register(\"  Bob.DOE_9  \", \"pw-value-1\".toCharArray()));\n        checkTrue(\"login through a different spelling of the same name\",\n            auth2.authenticate(\"bob.doe_9\", \"pw-value-1\".toCharArray()).isSuccess());\n        check(\"only one account exists\", true, auth2.accountExists(\"BOB.DOE_9\"));\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Section: lockout\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void lockout() {\n        AccountStore store = freshStore();\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(store, Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(3), log);\n        checkTrue(\"carol registered\", auth.register(\"carol\", PW));\n\n        for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++) {\n            UserAuthentication.Result r = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", (\"bad-choice-\" + i).toCharArray());\n            check(\"strike \" + i + \" is a credential failure\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, r.outcome());\n            check(\"strike \" + i + \" count\", i, r.failedAttempts());\n        }\n        UserAuthentication.Result tripping = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", \"bad-choice-3\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"the strike that trips the lock still reports bad credentials\",\n            Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, tripping.outcome());\n        check(\"and reports the full count\", 3, tripping.failedAttempts());\n\n        UserAuthentication.Result locked = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", PW);\n        check(\"correct password is refused while locked\", Outcome.ACCOUNT_LOCKED, locked.outcome());\n        check(\"locked reason\", UserAuthentication.Reason.ACCOUNT_LOCKED, locked.auditReason());\n        check(\"locked message\", \"Account temporarily locked. Try again later.\", locked.userMessage());\n        check(\"count stays at the threshold\", 3, store.failuresOf(\"carol\"));\n        checkTrue(\"lockout event logged\", log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.ACCOUNT_LOCKED_OUT) == 1);\n        checkTrue(\"locked attempt logged as a failure\", log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_FAILURE)\n            .reason == UserAuthentication.Reason.ACCOUNT_LOCKED);\n\n        checkTrue(\"operator unlock succeeds\", auth.unlock(\"carol\"));\n        check(\"counter cleared by the operator\", 0, store.failuresOf(\"carol\"));\n        checkTrue(\"login works after an operator unlock\", auth.authenticate(\"carol\", PW).isSuccess());\n        checkFalse(\"unlocking an unknown account reports false\", auth.unlock(\"nobody\"));\n\n        // A success must clear the strike counter, otherwise one verified login would be followed\n        // by a lock on the next honest typo.\n        UserAuthentication.Result s1 = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", \"slip-one\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"one strike after a verified login\", 1, s1.failedAttempts());\n        checkTrue(\"verified login again\", auth.authenticate(\"carol\", PW).isSuccess());\n        check(\"counter cleared by a successful login\", 0, store.failuresOf(\"carol\"));\n        UserAuthentication.Result after = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", \"slip-two\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"count restarts from one after a success\", 1, after.failedAttempts());\n        checkTrue(\"still not locked\", after.outcome() == Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);\n    }\n\n    private static void failureWindow() {\n        Instant t0 = Instant.parse(\"2026-09-12T10:00:00Z\");\n        AccountStore store = freshStore();\n        Config old = new Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(60),\n            Clock.fixed(t0, ZoneOffset.UTC));\n        UserAuthentication early = service(store, old, new RecordingLog());\n        checkTrue(\"window fixture registered\", early.register(\"dave\", PW));\n        for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++) {\n            early.authenticate(\"dave\", (\"old-\" + i + \"-failure\").toCharArray());\n        }\n        check(\"two strikes recorded at T0\", 2, store.failuresOf(\"dave\"));\n\n        Config later = new Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(60),\n            Clock.fixed(t0.plus(Duration.ofHours(2)), ZoneOffset.UTC));\n        UserAuthentication late = service(store, later, new RecordingLog());\n        UserAuthentication.Result r = late.authenticate(\"dave\", \"fresh-failure\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"lock expired by T0+2h so this is a credential failure\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n            r.outcome());\n        check(\"strikes outside the failure window were forgotten\", 1, r.failedAttempts());\n\n        // Strikes inside the window must accumulate, and the lock must hold for lockDuration.\n        Config soon = new Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(60),\n            Clock.fixed(t0.plus(Duration.ofMinutes(5)), ZoneOffset.UTC));\n        UserAuthentication mid = service(store, soon, new RecordingLog());\n        check(\"in-window strike accumulates\", 2, mid.authenticate(\"dave\", \"mid-window-1\".toCharArray()).failedAttempts());\n        UserAuthentication.Result third = mid.authenticate(\"dave\", \"mid-window-2\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"in-window third strike trips the lock\", 3, third.failedAttempts());\n        check(\"still reported as bad credentials\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, third.outcome());\n        check(\"now locked at T0+5m\", Outcome.ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n            mid.authenticate(\"dave\", PW).outcome());\n        Config stillLocked = new Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(60),\n            Clock.fixed(t0.plus(Duration.ofMinutes(19)), ZoneOffset.UTC));\n        check(\"lock holds until T0+20m\", Outcome.ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n            service(store, stillLocked, new RecordingLog()).authenticate(\"dave\", PW).outcome());\n        Config expired = new Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 3, Duration.ofMinutes(15), Duration.ofMinutes(60),\n            Clock.fixed(t0.plus(Duration.ofMinutes(21)), ZoneOffset.UTC));\n        check(\"auto-unlock once the lock duration has passed\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n            service(store, expired, new RecordingLog()).authenticate(\"dave\", PW).outcome());\n        check(\"counter reset by auto-unlock\", 0, store.failuresOf(\"dave\"));\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Section: disabled / corrupt accounts\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void disabledAndCorrupt() {\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), log);\n        checkTrue(\"erin registered\", auth.register(\"erin\", PW));\n        checkTrue(\"erin can be disabled\", auth.setEnabled(\"erin\", false));\n        UserAuthentication.Result off = auth.authenticate(\"erin\", PW);\n        check(\"disabled account refused\", Outcome.ACCOUNT_DISABLED, off.outcome());\n        check(\"disabled reason\", UserAuthentication.Reason.ACCOUNT_DISABLED, off.auditReason());\n        check(\"disabled account is not counted as a strike\", 0, off.failedAttempts());\n        checkTrue(\"disabled attempt logged\", log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_FAILURE)\n            .reason == UserAuthentication.Reason.ACCOUNT_DISABLED);\n        checkTrue(\"erin re-enabled\", auth.setEnabled(\"erin\", true));\n        checkTrue(\"erin can log in again\", auth.authenticate(\"erin\", PW).isSuccess());\n        checkFalse(\"disabling an unknown account reports false\", auth.setEnabled(\"nobody\", false));\n\n        CorruptStore store = new CorruptStore();\n        store.inject(UserAuthentication.Account.withCorruptHash(\"ghost\"));\n        UserAuthentication corrupt = service(store, Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), log);\n        UserAuthentication.Result broken = corrupt.authenticate(\"ghost\", PW);\n        check(\"account with an unreadable digest is refused\", Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n            broken.outcome());\n        check(\"and reported internally as a corrupt record\", UserAuthentication.Reason.CORRUPT_RECORD,\n            broken.auditReason());\n        checkTrue(\"a corrupt record is not a credential match\", !broken.isSuccess());\n\n        UserAuthentication noStore = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), log);\n        checkTrue(\"an empty store answers INVALID_INPUT for junk\",\n            noStore.authenticate(\"\", \"x\".toCharArray()).outcome() == Outcome.INVALID_INPUT);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Section: audit log\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void auditLog() throws IOException {\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(2), log);\n        auth.register(\"frank\", PW);\n\n        UserAuthentication.Result ok = auth.authenticate(\"frank\", PW, \"203.0.113.7\");\n        UserAuthentication.Event success = log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_SUCCESS);\n        check(\"success logged with the caller's address\", \"203.0.113.7\", success.remoteAddress);\n        check(\"success logged with the normalized user\", \"frank\", success.username);\n        check(\"success logged with the outcome\", Outcome.SUCCESS, success.outcome);\n        checkTrue(\"success records a duration\", ok.durationMillis() >= 0);\n\n        auth.authenticate(\"frank\", \"wrong-password\".toCharArray(), \"198.51.100.9\");\n        UserAuthentication.Event failure = log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_FAILURE);\n        check(\"failure logged with the caller's address\", \"198.51.100.9\", failure.remoteAddress);\n        check(\"failure logged with the reason\", UserAuthentication.Reason.BAD_PASSWORD, failure.reason);\n\n        auth.authenticate(\"nobody\", \"whatever-123\".toCharArray());\n        checkTrue(\"unknown user logged as a LOGIN_FAILURE with UNKNOWN_USER\",\n            log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_FAILURE).reason == UserAuthentication.Reason.UNKNOWN_USER);\n\n        auth.authenticate(\"bo\", \"whatever-123\".toCharArray());\n        checkTrue(\"malformed username logged as LOGIN_REFUSED\",\n            log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_REFUSED).reason\n                == UserAuthentication.Reason.MALFORMED_USERNAME);\n\n        auth.register(\"grace\", PW);\n        checkTrue(\"registration logged\",\n            log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.ACCOUNT_REGISTERED) == 2);\n        auth.unlock(\"grace\");\n        checkTrue(\"operator unlock logged\",\n            log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED) == 1);\n\n        for (UserAuthentication.Event event : log.events) {\n            checkFalse(\"log line is single line: \" + event.type, event.format().contains(\"\\n\"));\n            checkTrue(\"log line parses back: \" + event.type, UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(event.format()) != null);\n        }\n        checkTrue(\"every attempt produced exactly one LOGIN_* record\",\n            log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_SUCCESS)\n                + log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_FAILURE)\n                + log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_REFUSED) == 5);\n\n        String rendered = failure.format();\n        checkTrue(\"no credential ever appears in a log line\",\n            !rendered.contains(\"wrong-password\") && !rendered.contains(new String(PW)));\n        checkTrue(\"the salt/digest never appear either\", !rendered.contains(\"pbkdf2-sha256\"));\n\n        UserAuthentication.Event roundTrip = UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(rendered);\n        check(\"parseLine recovers the event type\", failure.type, roundTrip.type);\n        check(\"parseLine recovers the user\", failure.username, roundTrip.username);\n        check(\"parseLine recovers the reason\", failure.reason, roundTrip.reason);\n        check(\"parseLine recovers the address\", failure.remoteAddress, roundTrip.remoteAddress);\n        checkTrue(\"parseLine rejects junk\", UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(\"not a log line\") == null);\n        checkTrue(\"parseLine rejects null\", UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(null) == null);\n\n        Path file = Files.createTempFile(\"auth-audit\", \".log\");\n        try {\n            try (UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger fileLogger =\n                     new UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger(file, null)) {\n                UserAuthentication.State fileAuth = serviceForFileLogger(fileLogger);\n                fileAuth.register(\"henry\", PW);\n                fileAuth.authenticate(\"henry\", PW, \"203.0.113.10\");\n                fileAuth.authenticate(\"henry\", \"wrong-password\".toCharArray(), \"203.0.113.10\");\n                fileAuth.authenticate(\"nobody-at-all\", \"whatever-123\".toCharArray(), null);\n            }\n            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n            check(\"file logger wrote one flushed line per event\", 4, lines.size());\n            checkTrue(\"file lines are well formed\",\n                lines.stream().allMatch(l -> l.contains(\"|event=\") && !l.contains(\"\\r\")));\n            checkTrue(\"file logger records the failure reason\", lines.stream()\n                .anyMatch(l -> l.contains(\"reason=BAD_PASSWORD\")));\n            checkTrue(\"file logger records the success\", lines.stream()\n                .anyMatch(l -> l.contains(\"event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\")));\n            checkTrue(\"log file is ASCII only\", lines.stream().noneMatch(l -> !isAscii(l)));\n\n            try (UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger second =\n                     new UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger(file, null)) {\n                serviceForFileLogger(second).authenticate(\"henry\", PW);\n            }\n            check(\"a reopened logger appends instead of truncating\", 5,\n                Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).size());\n        } finally {\n            Files.deleteIfExists(file);\n        }\n\n        PrintStream originalOut = System.out;\n        ByteArrayOutputStream buffer = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        try {\n            System.setOut(new PrintStream(buffer, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            UserAuthentication consoleAuth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS),\n                new UserAuthentication.ConsoleEventLogger());\n            consoleAuth.register(\"ivy\", PW);\n            consoleAuth.authenticate(\"ivy\", PW);\n        } finally {\n            System.setOut(originalOut);\n        }\n        String consoleOutput = buffer.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        checkTrue(\"console logger prints the event\", consoleOutput.contains(\"event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\"));\n        checkTrue(\"console logger never prints the password\", !consoleOutput.contains(new String(PW)));\n\n        UserAuthentication quietAuth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS),\n            UserAuthentication.EventLogger.nullLogger());\n        checkTrue(\"null logger still authenticates\",\n            (quietAuth.register(\"jack\", PW) && quietAuth.authenticate(\"jack\", PW).isSuccess()));\n    }\n\n    private static UserAuthentication.State serviceForFileLogger(\n        UserAuthentication.EventLogger logger) {\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), logger);\n        return new UserAuthentication.State(auth);\n    }\n\n    private static void logInjection() {\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(freshStore(), Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS), log);\n        String[] hostile = {\n            \"mallory\\r\\n\" + \"2026-09-12T00:00:00Z|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS|user=admin|outcome=SUCCESS\",\n            \"bob|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS|user=admin\",\n            \"alice\\n|detail=pwned\",\n            \"a\".repeat(200) + \"|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\",\n            \"tabs\tand-control-\u001fchars\"\n        };\n        for (String name : hostile) {\n            UserAuthentication.Result r = auth.authenticate(name, \"whatever-123\".toCharArray());\n            checkTrue(\"hostile username refused: \" + r.auditReason(),\n                r.outcome() == Outcome.INVALID_INPUT || r.outcome() == Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);\n        }\n        for (UserAuthentication.Event event : log.events) {\n            checkTrue(\"no injected newline in a field\", !event.username.contains(\"\\n\")\n                && !event.username.contains(\"\\r\") && !event.detail.contains(\"\\n\"));\n            checkTrue(\"no injected delimiter in a field\", !event.username.contains(\"|\"));\n            checkTrue(\"field stays ASCII and short\", isAscii(event.username)\n                && event.username.length() <= 65);\n            check(\"formatted record still has exactly 8 delimiters\", 8,\n                countChar(event.format(), '|'));\n        }\n        checkTrue(\"sanitizeField neutralizes CRLF\",\n            !UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"a\\r\\nb\").contains(\"\\r\")\n                && !UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"a\\r\\nb\").contains(\"\\n\"));\n        check(\"sanitizeField replaces the delimiter\", \"a.b\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"a|b\"));\n        check(\"sanitizeField truncates with a marker\", 65,\n            UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"z\".repeat(300)).length());\n        check(\"sanitizeField marks absent values\", \"-\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(null));\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Section: equal work\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void equalWork() {\n        AccountStore store = freshStore();\n        UserAuthentication heavy = service(store, Config.fast(TIMING_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(NO_LOCKOUT),\n            new RecordingLog());\n        heavy.register(\"known-user\", \"known-user-password\".toCharArray());\n        heavy.register(\"malcolm\", \"malcolm-password\".toCharArray());\n        heavy.register(\"una\", \"una-password\".toCharArray());\n        heavy.register(\"peter\", \"peter-password\".toCharArray());\n\n        UserAuthentication.PasswordHash charge = heavy.equalWorkHash();\n        check(\"the equal-work digest is real and uses the configured rounds\", TIMING_ITERATIONS,\n            charge.iterations());\n        checkTrue(\"the equal-work digest is a full-length digest\", charge.salt().length == 16);\n        long chargeMillis = timeOnce(() -> charge.deriveKey(\n            \"equal-work-placeholder-never-a-real-secret\".toCharArray()));\n        checkTrue(\"the equal-work digest actually costs work: \" + chargeMillis + \"ms\",\n            chargeMillis >= 5);\n        checkTrue(\"no stored account can present the equal-work secret\",\n            !heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\",\n                \"equal-work-placeholder-never-a-real-secret\".toCharArray()).isSuccess());\n\n        AccountStore lockStore = freshStore();\n        UserAuthentication oneStrike = service(lockStore,\n            Config.fast(TIMING_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(1), new RecordingLog());\n        oneStrike.register(\"lock-a\", \"password-for-a\".toCharArray());\n        oneStrike.register(\"lock-b\", \"password-for-b\".toCharArray());\n        oneStrike.register(\"lock-c\", \"password-for-c\".toCharArray());\n        oneStrike.register(\"lock-d\", \"password-for-d\".toCharArray());\n        oneStrike.register(\"lock-e\", \"password-for-e\".toCharArray());\n\n        Series wrong = new Series() {\n            double run(UserAuthentication.Service s) {\n                return s.time(heavy::knownWrong);\n            }\n        };\n        // Each shape gets its own fresh account where a strike would otherwise poison later\n        // samples, so the five series stay comparable.\n        double knownWrong = timed(5, () -> heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double unknown = timed(5, () -> heavy.authenticate(\"no-such-user-x\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double badName = timed(5, () -> heavy.authenticate(\"bo\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double badPassword = timed(5, () -> heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\", new char[0]));\n        double locked = timed(5, () -> {\n            String name = \"lock-\" + \"abcde\".charAt(lockCursor++);\n            oneStrike.authenticate(name, \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray());\n            return oneStrike.authenticate(name, \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray());\n        });\n\n        checkTrue(\"a refused credential check costs real work: \" + (long) knownWrong + \"ms\",\n            knownWrong >= 15.0);\n        ratio(\"unknown user\", unknown, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"malformed username\", badName, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"unusable password candidate\", badPassword, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"locked account\", locked, knownWrong);\n        System.out.println(\"    [timing ms] known-wrong=\" + (long) knownWrong + \" unknown=\"\n            + (long) unknown + \" bad-name=\" + (long) badName + \" bad-password=\" + (long) badPassword\n            + \" locked=\" + (long) locked);\n    }\n\n    private static int lockCursor;\n\n    private interface Series {\n        double run(UserAuthentication.Service s);\n    }\n\n    private static double timed(int samples, Runnable attempt) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            attempt.run();\n        }\n        long[] observed = new long[samples];\n        for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++) {\n            long begin = System.nanoTime();\n            attempt.run();\n            observed[i] = (System.nanoTime() - begin) / 1_000_000L;\n        }\n        Arrays.sort(observed);\n        return observed[observed.length / 2];\n    }\n\n    private static void ratio(String label, double measured, double baseline) {\n        double r = measured / baseline;\n        checkTrue(label + \" costs comparable work (ratio \" + String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, \"%.2f\", r)\n            + \", want 0.6-1.7)\", r >= 0.6 && r <= 1.7);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Section: concurrency\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void concurrency() throws Exception {\n        final int threads = 8;\n        final int trials = 120;\n        int lost = 0;\n        for (int trial = 0; trial < trials; trial++) {\n            UserAuthentication.Account account = UserAuthentication.Account.withCorruptHash(\"race\");\n            Config config = Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(threads + 10);\n            CyclicBarrier barrier = new CyclicBarrier(threads);\n            CountDownLatch done = new CountDownLatch(threads);\n            for (int t = 0; t < threads; t++) {\n                new Thread(() -> {\n                    try {\n                        barrier.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                        account.recordFailure(Instant.now(), config);\n                    } catch (Exception failure) {\n                        record(\"race trial threw \" + failure);\n                    } finally {\n                        done.countDown();\n                    }\n                }).start();\n            }\n            done.await(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n            if (account.failedAttempts() != threads) {\n                lost++;\n            }\n        }\n        check(\"failure counter never loses an update (\" + trials + \" trials x \" + threads + \" threads)\",\n            0, lost);\n\n        UserAuthentication.Account cleared = UserAuthentication.Account.withCorruptHash(\"race2\");\n        Config bigWindow = Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS).withoutLockout(1000);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n            cleared.recordFailure(Instant.now(), bigWindow);\n        }\n        check(\"raced failures are all counted\", 4, cleared.failedAttempts());\n        check(\"success clears them all\", 4, cleared.recordSuccess(Instant.now()));\n\n        final int writers = 4;\n        final int perWriter = 25;\n        Path file = Files.createTempFile(\"auth-audit-concurrent\", \".log\");\n        try (UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger fileLogger =\n                 new UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger(file, null)) {\n            CyclicBarrier start = new CyclicBarrier(writers);\n            CountDownLatch finished = new CountDownLatch(writers);\n            for (int w = 0; w < writers; w++) {\n                final int id = w;\n                new Thread(() -> {\n                    try {\n                        start.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                        for (int i = 0; i < perWriter; i++) {\n                            fileLogger.log(newEvent(id, i));\n                        }\n                    } catch (Exception failure) {\n                        record(\"file logger writer threw \" + failure);\n                    } finally {\n                        finished.countDown();\n                    }\n                }).start();\n            }\n            finished.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n            check(\"concurrent writers keep every       RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(store, fastConfig().withoutLockout(3), log);\n        yes(\"carol registers\", auth.register(\"carol\", UserAuthentication.Result locked = auth.authenticate(\"carol\", PW);\n        eq(\"the right password is refused while locked\", UserAuthentication.Outcome.ACCOUNT_LOCKED,\n      auth.authenticate(\"carol\", PW);\n        eq(\"successes are logged too\", before + 1,\n            log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_SUCCESS));\n    }\n\n    private static void failureWindowAndExpiry() {\n        Instant t0 = Instant.parse(\"2026-09-12T10:00:00Z\");\n        InMemoryAccountStore store = new InMemoryAccountStore();\n\n        UserAuthentication window = Duration.ofMinutes(60), Clock.fixed(when, ZoneOffset.UTC));\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Disabled and damaged    eq(\"a malformed username is logged as a refusal\", UserAuthentication.Reason.MALFORMED_USERNAME,\n            log.last(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_REFUSED).reason);\n\n eq(\"the operator unlock is logged\", 1,\n            log.countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType.ACCOUNT_UNLOCKED));\n\n        for (UserAuthentication.Event event : log.snapshot()) {\n            no(\"line has no newline (\" + event.type + \")\", event.format().contains(\"\\n\"));\n            yes(\"line parses back (\" + event.type + \")\",\n fileAuth.authenticate(\"nobody-at-all\", \"whatever-123\".toCharArray(), null);\n            }\n            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n            eq(\"the file logger wrote one line per event\", 4, lines.size());\n            yes(\"every file line is a well formed record\", lines.stream()\n                .allMatch(line -> line.contains(\"|event=\") && UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(line) != null));\n            yes(\"the failure reason reached the file\", lines.stream()\n                .anyMatch(line -> yes(\"the file is pure ASCII\", lines.stream().noneMatch(line -> !isAscii(line)));\n\n            try (UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger reopened =\n                     new Files.deleteIfExists(file);\n        }\n\n        PrintStream originalOut = System.out;\n        ByteArrayOutputStream buffer = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        try {\n System.setOut(originalOut);\n        }\n        String printed = buffer.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        yes(\"the console logger prints the event\", quiet.authenticate(\"jack\", PW).isSuccess());\n    }\n\n    private static void logInjection() {\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(new InMemoryAccountStore(), fastConfig(), log);\n        auth.register(\"peggy\", PW);\n        String tab = String.valueOf((char) 0x09);\n        String[] hostile = {\n            \"mallory\\r\\n2026-09-12T00:00:00Z|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS|user=admin|outcome=SUCCESS\",\n            \"bob|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS|user=admin\",\n            \"alice\\n|detail=pwned\" + tab + \"x\",\n            \"a\".repeat(200) + \"|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\",\n            \"admin\" + (char) 0x00 + \"|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\",\n            \"user\" + (char) 0x1B + \"[31m\"\n        };\n        for (String name : hostile) {\n            UserAuthentication.Result r = auth.authenticate(name, \"whatever-123\".toCharArray());\n            yes(\"hostile username is refused [\" + r.auditReason() + \"]\",\n                r.outcome() == UserAuthentication.Outcome.INVALID_INPUT\n                    || r.outcome() == UserAuthentication.Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);\n        }\n        UserAuthentication.Result viaPassword =\n            auth.authenticate(\"peggy\", (\"pass\" + (char) 0x0A + \"|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS|x=y\").toCharArray());\n        eq(\"a newline inside the candidate password cannot forge a record\",\n            UserAuthentication.Outcome.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, viaPassword.outcome());\n\n        for (UserAuthentication.Event event : log.snapshot()) {\n            no(\"field carries no CR or LF\", event.username.contains(\"\\r\")\n                || event.username.contains(\"\\n\") || event.detail.contains(\"\\n\"));\n            no(\"field carries no delimiter\", event.username.contains(\"|\") || event.detail.contains(\"|\"));\n            yes(\"field is ASCII and bounded\", isAscii(event.username) && event.username.length() <= 65);\n            eq(\"record keeps its eight delimiters\", 8, countChar(event.format(), '|'));\n            eq(\"record still parses\", true, UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(event.format()) != null);\n        }\n        eq(\"sanitizeField neutralizes CRLF\", \"a.b\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"a\\r\\nb\"));\n        eq(\"sanitizeField replaces the delimiter\", \"a.b\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"a|b\"));\n        eq(\"sanitizeField marks truncation\", 65, UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"z\".repeat(300)).length());\n        eq(\"sanitizeField marks a missing value\", \"-\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(null));\n        eq(\"sanitizeField marks an empty value\", \"-\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"\"));\n        eq(\"sanitizeField keeps ordinary text\", \"203.0.113.7\", UserAuthentication.sanitizeField(\"203.0.113.7\"));\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Equal work\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void equalWork() {\n        UserAuthentication heavy = service(new InMemoryAccountStore(),\n            new UserAuthentication.Config(TIMING_ITERATIONS, NO_LOCKOUT, Duration.ofMinutes(15),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(30), Clock.systemUTC()), new RecordingLog());\n        heavy.register(\"known-user\", \"known-user-password\".toCharArray());\n\n        UserAuthentication.PasswordHash charge = heavy.equalWorkHash();\n        eq(\"the equal-work digest uses the configured rounds\", TIMING_ITERATIONS, charge.iterations());\n        eq(\"the equal-work digest has a full salt\", 16, charge.salt().length);\n        long chargeMillis = timeOnce(() -> charge.deriveKey(EQUAL_WORK_TEXT.toCharArray()));\n        yes(\"the equal-work digest really costs work: \" + chargeMillis + \"ms\", chargeMillis >= 5);\n        no(\"no account accepts the equal-work secret as its password\",\n            heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\", EQUAL_WORK_TEXT.toCharArray()).isSuccess());\n        yes(\"the equal-work digest verifies its own derivation\",\n            charge.matches(charge.deriveKey(EQUAL_WORK_TEXT.toCharArray())));\n\n        // One-strike service so each locked sample can be prepared on a fresh account: per-account\n        // lockout would otherwise short-circuit later samples and fabricate the ratio.\n        UserAuthentication oneStrike = service(new InMemoryAccountStore(),\n            new UserAuthentication.Config(TIMING_ITERATIONS, 1, Duration.ofMinutes(15),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(30), Clock.systemUTC()), new RecordingLog());\n        for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {\n            oneStrike.register(\"lock-\" + i, \"password-for-lock-\" + i);\n        }\n        final int[] cursor = {0};\n\n        double knownWrong = timed(5, () ->\n            heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double unknown = timed(5, () ->\n            heavy.authenticate(\"no-such-user-x\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double badName = timed(5, () ->\n            heavy.authenticate(\"bo\", \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray()));\n        double badPassword = timed(5, () -> heavy.authenticate(\"known-user\", new char[0]));\n        double disabledCase = disabledBranchMillis(TIMING_ITERATIONS);\n        double locked = timed(5, () -> {\n            String name = \"lock-\" + (cursor[0]++ % 16);\n            oneStrike.authenticate(name, \"the-wrong-password\".toCharArray());\n            return oneStrike.authenticate(name, \"password-for-lock-\" + (cursor[0] - 1) % 16).outcome()\n                == UserAuthentication.Outcome.ACCOUNT_LOCKED;\n        });\n\n        yes(\"a refused credential check costs real work: \" + (long) knownWrong + \"ms\",\n            knownWrong >= 8.0);\n        ratio(\"unknown user\", unknown, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"malformed username\", badName, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"unusable password candidate\", badPassword, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"disabled account\", disabledCase, knownWrong);\n        ratio(\"locked account\", locked, knownWrong);\n        System.out.println(\"    [median ms] known-wrong=\" + (long) knownWrong + \" unknown=\"\n            + (long) unknown + \" bad-name=\" + (long) badName + \" bad-password=\" + (long) badPassword\n            + \" disabled=\" + (long) disabledCase + \" locked=\" + (long) locked);\n    }\n\n    private static double disabledBranchMillis(int iterations) {\n        UserAuthentication service = service(new InMemoryAccountStore(),\n            new UserAuthentication.Config(iterations, NO_LOCKOUT, Duration.ofMinutes(15),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(30), Clock.systemUTC()), new RecordingLog());\n        service.register(\"off-user\", \"off-user-password\".toCharArray());\n        service.setEnabled(\"off-user\", false);\n        return timed(5, () -> service.authenticate(\"off-user\", \"anything-at-all\".toCharArray()));\n    }\n\n    /** Median of {@code samples} calls, after two warm-up calls, timed with the wall clock. */\n    private static double timed(int samples, java.util.function.Supplier<?> attempt) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            attempt.get();\n        }\n        long[] observed = new long[samples];\n        for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++) {\n            long begin = System.nanoTime();\n            attempt.get();\n            observed[i] = (System.nanoTime() - begin) / 1_000_000L;\n        }\n        Arrays.sort(observed);\n        return observed[observed.length / 2];\n    }\n\n    private static void ratio(String label, double measured, double baseline) {\n        double value = measured / baseline;\n        yes(label + \" costs comparable work (ratio \" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"%.2f\", value)\n            + \", want 0.6-1.7)\", value >= 0.6 && value <= 1.7);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Concurrency\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void concurrency() throws Exception {\n        final int threads = 8;\n        final int trials = 100;\n        int lost = 0;\n        UserAuthentication.Config config = fastConfig().withoutLockout(threads + 10);\n        for (int trial = 0; trial < trials; trial++) {\n            UserAuthentication.Account account = UserAuthentication.Account.withCorruptHash(\"race-\" + trial);\n            CyclicBarrier barrier = new CyclicBarrier(threads);\n            CountDownLatch done = new CountDownLatch(threads);\n            for (int t = 0; t < threads; t++) {\n                new Thread(() -> {\n                    try {\n                        barrier.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                        account.recordFailure(Instant.now(), config);\n                    } catch (Exception failure) {\n                        record(\"strike racer threw \" + failure);\n                    } finally {\n                        done.countDown();\n                    }\n                }).start();\n            }\n            done.await(20, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n            if (account.failedAttempts() != threads) {\n                lost++;\n            }\n        }\n        eq(\"the strike counter never loses an update (\" + trials + \" trials x \" + threads + \" threads)\",\n            0, lost);\n\n        UserAuthentication.Account mixed = UserAuthentication.Account.withCorruptHash(\"mixed\");\n        UserAuthentication.Config bigWindow = fastConfig().withoutLockout(1000);\n        CyclicBarrier barrier = new CyclicBarrier(12);\n        CountDownLatch done = new CountDownLatch(12);\n        for (int t = 0; t < 8; t++) {\n            new Thread(() -> {\n                try {\n                    barrier.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                    mixed.recordFailure(Instant.now(), bigWindow);\n                } catch (Exception failure) {\n                    record(\"mixed racer threw \" + failure);\n                } finally {\n                    done.countDown();\n                }\n            }).start();\n        }\n        for (int t = 0; t < 4; t++) {\n            new Thread(() -> {\n                try {\n                    barrier.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                    mixed.recordSuccess(Instant.now());\n                } catch (Exception failure) {\n                    record(\"mixed racer threw \" + failure);\n                } finally {\n                    done.countDown();\n                }\n            }).start();\n        }\n        done.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        yes(\"concurrent strikes and successes leave a consistent counter (got \"\n            + mixed.failedAttempts() + \")\", mixed.failedAttempts() <= 8);\n\n        final int writers = 4;\n        final int perWriter = 25;\n        Path file = Files.createTempFile(\"auth-audit-concurrent\", \".log\");\n        try (UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger fileLogger =\n                 new UserAuthentication.FileEventLogger(file, null)) {\n            CyclicBarrier start = new CyclicBarrier(writers);\n            CountDownLatch finished = new CountDownLatch(writers);\n            for (int w = 0; w < writers; w++) {\n                final int id = w;\n                new Thread(() -> {\n                    try {\n                        start.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                        for (int i = 0; i < perWriter; i++) {\n                            fileLogger.log(newEvent(id, i));\n                        }\n                    } catch (Exception failure) {\n                        record(\"file logger writer threw \" + failure);\n                    } finally {\n                        finished.countDown();\n                    }\n                }).start();\n            }\n            finished.await(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n            List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);\n            eq(\"every concurrent log line survived\", writers * perWriter, lines.size());\n            yes(\"no interleaved or partial lines\", lines.stream()\n                .allMatch(line -> UserAuthentication.Event.parseLine(line) != null));\n            eq(\"each writer kept its own lines\", writers * perWriter, (int) lines.stream()\n                .filter(line -> line.contains(\"|event=LOGIN_SUCCESS\")).count());\n        } finally {\n            Files.deleteIfExists(file);\n        }\n\n        final int racers = 16;\n        InMemoryAccountStore store = new InMemoryAccountStore();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(store, fastConfig().withoutLockout(NO_LOCKOUT), new RecordingLog());\n        auth.register(\"racist-target\", \"raced-password\".toCharArray());\n        CyclicBarrier loginBarrier = new CyclicBarrier(racers);\n        CountDownLatch loginDone = new CountDownLatch(racers);\n        AtomicInteger successes = new AtomicInteger();\n        for (int t = 0; t < racers; t++) {\n            new Thread(() -> {\n                try {\n                    loginBarrier.await(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n                    if (auth.authenticate(\"racist-target\", \"raced-password\".toCharArray()).isSuccess()) {\n                        successes.incrementAndGet();\n                    }\n                } catch (Exception failure) {\n                    record(\"login racer threw \" + failure);\n                } finally {\n                    loginDone.countDown();\n                }\n            }).start();\n        }\n        loginDone.await(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        eq(\"concurrent correct logins all succeed\", racers, successes.get());\n        eq(\"and all are logged\", racers, auth.loggedCount(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_SUCCESS));\n    }\n\n    private static UserAuthentication.Event newEvent(int id, int seq) {\n        return new UserAuthentication.Event(UserAuthentication.EventType.LOGIN_SUCCESS, \"writer-\" + id,\n            UserAuthentication.Outcome.SUCCESS, UserAuthentication.Reason.OK, \"10.0.0.\" + id, seq, 0,\n            \"seq \" + seq);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // API contract\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void apiContract() {\n        UserAuthentication.Config.defaults();\n        throwsA(\"absurdly low iteration count\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(10, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(1), Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"zero maxFailures\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 0, Duration.ofMinutes(1),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"negative maxFailures\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, -1, Duration.ofMinutes(1),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"zero lock duration\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ZERO,\n                Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"negative lock duration\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ofSeconds(-30),\n                Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"null lock duration\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, null, Duration.ofMinutes(1), null));\n        throwsA(\"zero failure window\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(1),\n                Duration.ZERO, null));\n        throwsA(\"null failure window\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication.Config(FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(1), null, null));\n        throwsA(\"null logger\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication(new InMemoryAccountStore(), null, fastConfig()));\n        throwsA(\"null store\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication(null, new RecordingLog(), fastConfig()));\n        throwsA(\"null config\", IllegalArgumentException.class,\n            () -> new UserAuthentication(new InMemoryAccountStore(), new RecordingLog(), null));\n        yes(\"a null clock falls back to the system clock\", new UserAuthentication.Config(\n            FAST_ITERATIONS, 5, Duration.ofMinutes(1), Duration.ofMinutes(1), null).clock != null);\n\n        RecordingLog log = new RecordingLog();\n        UserAuthentication auth = service(new InMemoryAccountStore(), fastConfig(), log);\n        auth.register(\"kate\", PW);\n        UserAuthentication.Result ok = auth.authenticate(\"  KATE \", PW, \"2001:db8::1\");\n        eq(\"the result echoes the normalized username\", \"kate\", ok.username());\n        yes(\"the result reports a duration\", ok.durationMillis() >= 0);\n        yes(\"toString names the outcome\", ok.toString().contains(\"SUCCESS\"));\n        no(\"toString leaks the password\", ok.toString().contains(new String(PW)));\n        no(\"an echoed invalid username cannot carry a delimiter\",\n            auth.authenticate(\"|admin|\", PW).username().contains(\"|\"));\n        eq(\"the echoed invalid username is sanitized\", \"-\", auth.authenticate(\"\", PW).username());\n\n        List<String> messages = new ArrayList<>();\n        messages.add(auth.authenticate(\"kate\", PW).userMessage());\n        messages.add(auth.authenticate(\"kate\", \"not-the-password\".toCharArray()).userMessage());\n        messages.add(auth.authenticate(\"ku\", PW).userMessage());\n        auth.setEnabled(\"kate\", false);\n        messages.add(auth.authenticate(\"kate\", PW).userMessage());\n        auth.setEnabled(\"kate\", true);\n        eq(\"every outcome shape has a message\", 4, messages.stream()\n            .filter(message -> message != null && !message.isEmpty()).count());\n        eq(\"success and failure wording differ\", false,\n            messages.get(0).equals(messages.get(1)));\n        yes(\"the log saw all of it\", log.snapshot().size() >= 8);\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Fixtures\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static UserAuthentication.Config fastConfig() {\n        return UserAuthentication.Config.fast(FAST_ITERATIONS);\n    }\n\n    private static UserAuthentication service(UserAuthentication.AccountStore store,\n                                              UserAuthentication.Config config) {\n        return service(store, config, new RecordingLog());\n    }\n\n    private static UserAuthentication service(UserAuthentication.AccountStore store,\n                                              UserAuthentication.Config config,\n                                              UserAuthentication.EventLogger logger) {\n        return new UserAuthentication(store, logger, config);\n    }\n\n    /** Store seam that can hand out an account whose stored digest is unreadable. */\n    private static final class CorruptStore implements UserAuthentication.AccountStore {\n        private final UserAuthentication.InMemoryAccountStore backing =\n            new UserAuthentication.InMemoryAccountStore();\n        private final Map<String, UserAuthentication.Account> injected = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n\n        void inject(UserAuthentication.Account account) {\n            injected.put(account.username(), account);\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public UserAuthentication.Account find(String username) {\n            UserAuthentication.Account account = injected.get(username);\n            return account != null ? account : backing.find(username);\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public boolean register(String username, UserAuthentication.PasswordHash hash) {\n            return backing.register(username, hash);\n        }\n\n        @Override\n        public int failuresOf(String username) {\n            UserAuthentication.Account account = injected.get(username);\n            return account != null ? account.failedAttempts() : backing.failuresOf(username);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Keeps every event in memory, in order. */\n    private static final class RecordingLog implements UserAuthentication.EventLogger {\n        private final List<UserAuthentication.Event> events =\n            Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());\n\n        @Override\n        public void log(UserAuthentication.Event event) {\n            events.add(event);\n        }\n\n        List<UserAuthentication.Event> snapshot() {\n            synchronized (events) {\n                return new ArrayList<>(events);\n            }\n        }\n\n        int countOf(UserAuthentication.EventType type) {\n            synchronized (events) {\n                return (int) events.stream().filter(event -> event.type == type).count();\n            }\n        }\n\n        UserAuthentication.Event last(UserAuthentication.EventType type) {\n            synchronized (events) {\n                for (int i = events.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n                    if (events.get(i).type == type) {\n                        return events.get(i);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            throw new AssertionError(\"no \" + type + \" event was logged\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Assertions\n    // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void section(String title) {\n        sectionNumber++;\n        System.out.println();\n        System.out.println(\"== \" + sectionNumber + \". \" + title);\n    }\n\n    private static void eq(String what, Object expected, Object actual) {\n        count();\n        if (expected == null ? actual == null : expected.equals(actual)) {\n            pass(what);\n        } else {\n            record(what + \" (expected [\" + expected + \"] but was [\" + actual + \"])\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void yes(String what, boolean actual) {\n        eq(what, Boolean.TRUE, actual);\n    }\n\n    private static void no(String what, boolean actual) {\n        eq(what, Boolean.FALSE, actual);\n    }\n\n    private static void nul(String what, String actual) {\n        eq(what + \" rejected\", null, actual);\n    }\n\n    private static void throwsA(String what, Class<? extends Throwable> expected, ThrowingCall call) {\n        count();\n        try {\n            call.run();\n            record(what + \" (expected \" + expected.getSimpleName() + \", nothing thrown)\");\n        } catch (Throwable thrown) {\n            if (expected.isInstance(thrown)) {\n                pass(what);\n            } else {\n                record(what + \" (expected \" + expected.getSimpleName() + \" but was \"\n                    + thrown.getClass().getName() + \")\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private interface ThrowingCall {\n        void run() throws Exception;\n    }\n\n    private static long timeOnce(Runnable body) {\n        long begin = System.nanoTime();\n        body.run();\n        return (System.nanoTime() - begin) / 1_000_000L;\n    }\n\n    private static int countChar(String value, char needle) {\n        int count = 0;\n        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n            if (value.charAt(i) == needle) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isAscii(String value) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n            if (value.charAt(i) > 0x7F) {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private static void count() {\n
